NATIONAL COMPETITIVE BIDDING for Setting up of Real Time Data Acquisition System, Hydro Modelling Flood Control Room at Ezhilagam and Flood Monitoring Centres for Water Resources Department Headquarters at Chepauk and four Collectorates

(Two-Envelope Bidding Process with e-Procurement)

INVITATION FOR BIDS (IFB)

E-Procurement Notice

                                    Date                : 18-07-2022

                                    Loan No.        : 84880- IN                                         

                                    IFB No.          : CRTFF / WRD / 01 / 2022                

1. The Government of India has received/applied/ intends to apply for financing from the World Bank towards the cost of “Planning, Setting up and Operationalizing a Real Time Flood Forecasting and Spatial Decision Support System for Adyar, Cooum, Kosasthalayar, Nagariyar, Nandhiyar and Kovalam Basins”, referred as Chennai Real Time Flood Forecasting & Spatial Decision Support System. It is intended that part of the proceeds will be applied to eligible payments under the contract for “Setting up of Real Time Data Acquisition System, Hydro Modelling Flood Control Room at Ezhilagam and Flood Monitoring Centres for Water Resources Department Headquarters at Chepauk and four Collectorates””

2. On behalf of the Governor of Tamil Nadu TheSuperintending Engineer, WRD, Groundwater Circle, Chennai-113 (Purchaser) now invites online bids from eligible Bidders for “Setting up of Real Time Data Acquisition System, Hydro Modelling Flood Control Room at Ezhilagam and Flood Monitoring Centres for Water Resources Department Headquarters at Chepauk and four Collectorates”.

3. Bidding will be conducted through the National Competitive Bidding (NCB) procedures agreed with World Bank. The bidding is open to all eligible Bidders as defined in the Bank’s Procurement Guidelines. In addition, please refer to paragraphs 1.6 and 1.7 of the Guidelines setting forth the World Bank’s policy on conflict of interest.

4. Bidding documents are available online on https://tntenders.gov.in for free.  Bidders will be required to register in the website, which is free of cost. The Bidder would be responsible for ensuring that any addenda available on the website is also downloaded and incorporated.

Free

5. For submission of the Bids, the Bidder is required to have Digital Signature Certificate (DSC) from one of the authorized Certifying Authorities, authorised by Government of India for issuing DSC. Aspiring Bidders who have not obtained the user ID and password for participating in e-procurement in this Project, may obtain the same from the website: https://tntenders.gov.in.

6. Bids must be submitted online on https://tntenders.gov.in on or before the date and time for receipt of bids, and the ‘Technical Part’ of Bids will be opened online on the specified time and date for opening of Bids, as given above. The “Financial Part” shall remain unopened in the e-procurement system until the second public Bid opening for the financial part. Any Bid or modifications to Bid (including discount) received outside e-procurement system will not be considered. If the office happens to be closed on the date of opening of the bids as specified, the bids will be opened on the next working day at the same time. The electronic bidding system would not allow any late submission of Bids.

7. Bids along with necessary enclosures must be uploaded to the web site https://tntenders.gov.in as per bid document and bids will be opened at the specified venue on the stipulated date and time, in the presence of the bidders or their authorized representatives who wish to attend. If the office happens to be closed on the date of opening of the bids as specified, the bids will be opened on the next working day at the same time and venue.

8. All bids must be accompanied by a (a) Bid-Securing Declaration, (b) Power of Attorney authorizing the signatory of the Bid and (c) Original Affidavit regarding correctness of information furnished with Bid Document, in approved form and shall be uploaded as scanned copy of the original document along with the Technical Part of the Bid.

9. Pre bid meeting will be held on 04-08-2022 at 11:00 AM at the Conference Hall, near the chambers of theSuperintending Engineer, Water Resources Department, Ground Water Circle, CSIR Road, Taramani, Chennai, TamilNadu – 600113 to clarify the issues and to answer questions on any matter that may be raised at that stage in ITB on clause 7.1 of instructions to bidders of the bidding document.

10. Other details can be seen in the Bidding Document. The Purchaser shall not be held liable for any delays due to system failure beyond its control. Even though the system will attempt to notify the Bidders of any Bid updates, the Purchaser shall not be liable for any information not received by the Bidder. It is the Bidders’ responsibility to verify the website for the latest information related to this Bid.

    4. \n\t

Brief Scope of Work and key details for Preparation of Next Generation of Urban Sector Interventions for various Urban Local Bodies excluding Chennai Metropolitan Area in Tamil Nadu:

  1. OBJECTIVES
    1. To prepare a Comprehensive Statewide Urban Assessment Report for various Corporations, and Municipalities in Tamil Nadu excluding Chennai Metropolitan Area, based on needs of the ULBs.
    2. To study, analyse and prepare a list of sustainable and bankable urban infrastructure sub-projects with an Action Plan based on the demand for the need-based projects in the short term (within 5 years), medium term (within 15 years) and long-term plan (beyond 15 years) for the variable Urban Local Bodies. This shall cover the following sub-sectors, viz. water-supply, sewerage, solid waste management, urban transportation, urban roads, streetlights, Bus Terminal, Bus Stops, MLCP, MLTWP, lake rejuvenation, waterbodies vested with ULB, etc.
    3. The identified ULB-wise, priority sub-projects shall lead to a pipeline of sub-projects for subsequent preparation of Project Reports like Detailed Project Reports (DPRs), Feasibility Reports, etc., for various ULBs by appointing consultants as per World Bank procurement guidelines.
  2. STUDY AREA

The study area consists of all Urban Local Bodies in Tamil Nadu excluding the Chennai Metropolitan Area. The study shall necessarily cover all the 121 Municipalities across 7 Regions and 14 City Municipal Corporations. This 135 ULBs are administered by Commissioner of Municipal Administration (CMA). Further, CMA is administering the Municipalities through Regional Directorate of Municipal Administration (RDMA) across 7 regions. List of ULBs – All Municipalities & Corporations (Except Greater Chennai Corporation).

3. SCOPE OF STUDY AND TASKS

The scope of the study are as follows:

    1. The Urban Assessment Report shall be formulated by reviewing the existing documents, state level urban sector policies, GOs, GoTN Vision document, CDPs/CCBPs/CMPs various State Finance Commission’s reports, review of DPRs, Master Plans, prepared by ULBs and having discussions with the various entities involved in urban management in Tamil Nadu.
    2. The urban assessment will also analyse and take into account various state and central level urban schemes, including SMART Cities, Swachh Bharat, AMRUT, etc., with their results and impacts achieved till date in the urban sector in TN.
    3. The next generation of projects should take into account all urban projects being implemented at ULBs, in pipeline and source of funding (both planned and proposed), wherever required propose cluster-based approach for urban infrastructure projects.
    4. This should include a gap analysis for each of the sectors and for each ULBs with an aim to capture the service up-gradations required for the ULBs and its residents (including suggestion for improvements in O&M to achieve desired service levels). The consultant’s analysis should include sector-wise summary indicators for the urban sector in the state as a whole as well as for different categories of ULBs.
    1. Prepare a Urban Assessment Report with an action plan for each of the participating ULBs, considering Population, Status of its urban infrastructure projects and services including the following, (but not limited to):
    1. Water Supply, (Automation, 24/7, real time water quality monitoring, SCADA)
    2. Wastewater (Network & Treatment including septage management, reuse and recycle),
    3. Storm Water Drainage System (including potential for rainwater harvesting and lake rejuvenation),
    4. Solid Waste Management (including potential for Construction & Demolition waste, hazardous waste infrastructure).
    5. Urban Transportation: Urban Roads, Bridges Ecofriendly & Energy saving streetlights, Bus Terminal, Bus Stops, MLCP, MLTWP
    6. Other innovative projects such as IOT based real time monitoring (some possible options are real time flood forecasting and spatial decision support system for flood prone cities)
    7. GIS based asset management system with an aim to manage infrastructure by minimizing the total cost of owning, operating and maintaining assets at acceptable levels of service).
    8. Efficient O&M Model for recently completed/ongoing projects.
    1. The consultant is also expected:
    1. To articulate a clear ‘Vision’ for Urban Sector / ULBs in Tamil Nadu based on consultations with GoTN as well as external stakeholders, including experts, think-tanks, etc.
    2. To assess the issues and challenges in urban infrastructure development and Operation & Maintenance implementation, cost recovery and sustainability.
    3. This study shall clearly bring out the status of key urban sector indicators in TN and the strategy / roadmap to demand-supply gap in urban services over time. Such indicators shall be presented sector-wise for the urban sector in the state as a whole as well as for different categories of ULBs with supporting analytics.
    4. To estimate the investment needs for the urban sector in Tamil Nadu as whole as well as ULB-wise for the next 20 years.
    5. To assess the financial assessment of the ULBs in a wholistic manner and broad investment / borrowing capacity for each of the ULBs for the identified sub-projects.
    6. To assess and classify the ULBs in terms of growth potential.
    7. To suggest mechanisms for effective implementation of the Urban Assessment Plan.
    8. Include an action plan justifying the needs and benefits to the various stakeholders like general public, ULBs, Government, etc., with economic benefits, financial benefits, social benefits, environmental benefits, etc. and overall Measures to address common growth and infrastructure issues under the purview of the ULBs.
    9. To include specific timeframes and provisional outcome-based benchmarks for implementation each of the projects identified and each of the ULBs in all aspects.   
    10. To conduct Worksop in seven regions as per Regional Director of Municipal Administration (RDMA) set up covering City Municipal Corporations & Municipalities.
    1. The pipeline of ULB-wise, priority sub-projects shall be provided with block cost estimates with necessary prioritisation and phasing plan. Proposals shall include priority investments required for restoring required service levels in minimum time and minimum cost.
    2. The list of feasible and implementable sub-projects shall be identified in consultation with the ULBs, other key stakeholders in the city and the same shall be assessed for preliminary technical, financial, environmental, social, legal, regulatory, sustainability, etc.,
    3. The identified sub-projects shall also include interventions that bring a strong urban service delivery orientation at ULBs through outsourcing / performance / O&M Contracts (such as for moving towards 24X7 water-supply, collection & transportation contracts in Solid Waste Management, etc.)
    4. For the identified sub-projects, the consultants shall prepare an one page flier with salient details including key map for various ULBs.
    5. The identified and prioritised sub-projects shall be provided with sufficient basic details so as to lead to preparation of Detailed Projects Reports / Feasibility Reports / Transaction Advisory / etc., by the Urban Local Bodies. Issues in implementation such as (i) Availability of land for treatment plants, ROW for pipelines / sewers/drains, (ii) Environmental clearance (iii) Social acceptance, etc need to be broadly identified.
    6. The Consultant shall also provide draft TORs for preparation of DPRs (including social, environmental and other assessments as necessary) for the prioritised / implementable (first batch) of sub-projects.

4. KEY PERSONNEL (Tentative) – [Final required Key staffs will be mentioned in Terms of Reference.]

      1. Team leader / Urban Expert
      2. Urban Finance and Management specialist
      3. Urban Infrastructure specialist/ Municipal Engineer
      4. Program Managers
      5. Environmental and Social Safeguards Specialist
      6. Public Communication Specialist
      7. Digital Development Specialist

TAMIL NADU URBAN INFRASTRUCTURE FINANCIAL SERVICES LIMITED

\r\n\r\n

REQUEST FOR EXPRESSION OF INTEREST (REOI)

\r\n\r\n

(Firms are invited)

\r\n\r\n

TAMIL NADU SUSTAINABLE URBAN DEVELOPMENT PROJECT (TNSUDP)

\r\n\r\n

CONSULTING SERVICES

\r\n\r\n

 Loan No: 8488 IN. Project No. P150395

\r\n\r\n

The Government of India has received a loan from the International Bank for Reconstruction and Development (IBRD) and intends to apply part of the proceeds to make the payments under the contract for the consultancy services detailed below:

\r\n\r\n

Chennai City and surrounding regions gets 75-80-% of the rainfall in three months period between October to December. In the last 50 years, catastrophic floods occurred in Chennai and upstream /surrounding areas during the years of 1976, 1985, 1996, 2005 and 2015. These floods have caused heavy damages to infrastructure of the city and loss of lives and they were severe along the waterways stretches of Adyar, Cooum, Kosasthalayar, Buckingham Canal and also in Pallikaranai Marshland in Kovalam basin. It is envisaged to develop a system that would facilitate operations of the water bodies to act on time and prepare stakeholder and departments for the floods and also forecasting the river flow and mapping of potential flood zone in advance will help in taking the decisions such as evacuation of the likely affecting areas well in advance.

\r\n\r\n

In view of the above, the Government of Tamil Nadu through Commissionerate of Revenue Administration (CRA) and Greater Chennai Corporation (GCC) envisages setup a Flood Control Room as part of Decision Support System and also setup instrumentation, updating the Hydrological model based on real time data from equipment (Automatic Weather Station & Automatic Water Level Recorders at Rivers) at State Emergency Operating Centre in CRA and Rippon Building, Greater Chennai Corporation. This Decision Support system will be set up in Two years time and will provide early warning of river estimated flow of water in Adyar, Cooum, Kosasthalayar and Buckingham Canal. Further, it also will indicate the street level flooding across the city based on the rainfall forecast.

\r\n\r\n

In this regard, TNUIFSL invites eligible Consultancy firm(s) / System Integrators having experience and expertise in the field of developing projects and programmes in the sector listed below:

Consulting Services for Planning, Setting up and Operationalizing a Real Time Flood Forecasting and Spatial Decision Support System for Adyar, Cooum, Kosasthalayar and Kovalam Basins and setting up of a Hydro Modelling cum Flood Monitoring Control Room at State Emergency Operation Centre, Chennai, setting up of Flood Monitoring Control Rooms at Greater Chennai Corporation, Kancheepuram and Tiruvallur Collectorate

\r\n\t\t\t

Interested firms must provide information as indicated in the format that they are qualified to perform the services (along with brochures, description of similar assignments, experience in similar conditions, availability of appropriate skills among staff, etc., if any).

\r\n\r\n

If the firms are bidding jointly, a binding declaration must be given of the lead firm and what form the cooperation will take (joint venture or sub-contracting). Interested firm(s) must provide all the relevant information (in English language only).  A consultant will be selected in accordance with the procedures set out in the World Bank’s Procurement Regulations for IPF Borrowers - Procurement in Investment Project Financing Goods, Works, Non-Consulting and Consulting Services [July 2016].

\r\n\r\n

The attention of interested Consultants is drawn to paragraph 3.14 to 3.17 of the World Bank’s Procurement Regulations for IPF Borrowers - Procurement in Investment Project Financing Goods, Works, Non-Consulting and Consulting Services [July 2016] setting forth the World Bank’s policy on conflict of interest. 

\r\n\r\n

Separate EoI's shall be submitted by the firms for each of the assignments.  Interested consultants may apply for the EOI as per the format prescribed (format can be downloaded from the websites www.tenders.tn.gov.in and www.tnuifsl.com) and may obtain further information at the address below during office hours.

\r\n\r\n

“Expression of interest must be delivered in Hard copy along with soft copy in a CD to the address below by 02.06.2017, with a superscribing “Empanelling Consultants for Consulting Services for Planning, Setting up and Operationalizing a Real Time Flood Forecasting and Spatial Decision Support System for Adyar, Cooum, Kosasthalayar and Kovalam Basins and setting up of a Hydro Modelling cum Flood Monitoring Control Room at State Emergency Operation Centre, Chennai, setting up of Flood Monitoring Control Rooms at Greater Chennai Corporation, Kancheepuram and Tiruvallur Collectorate”.

\r\n\r\n

Proposals without superscribing in the cover of the proposals as requested above will be returned unopened.

Empanellement :

\r\n\r\n
    \r\n\t
  1. Separate proposals have to be submitted for each of the sectors.
    2. \r\n\t
  2. The consultants will be ranked and empanelled in each sector. As and when assignment comes, the top six firms will be issued RFP. In the case any one of the firm’s declines to participate in submitting their proposals, the next firm in the order of their ranking will be replaced.
    3. \r\n\t
  3. Subsequently, for further packages (if any), the next ranked firms will be issued RFP (not necessarily for the Top Six firms) such that all empanelled firms get equal opportunity to participate in the bids.
    4. \r\n\t
  4. The firms which have won at-least one award in the mentioned sector will be not included in the subsequent packages.
    5. \r\n\t
  5. In the case of firms applying as Joint Venture, both the firm should have atleast minimum one experience in the applied sector. Else, the firm which has experience alone will be empanelled.
    6. \r\n\t
  6. In the case of parent company claiming experience of subsidiary firm and vice versa. The empanellement will be done considering both Parent company and subsidiary as a Joint Venture Entity.
    7. \r\n
\r\n\r\n

The attention of interested Consultants is drawn to paragraph 3.14 to 3.17 of the World Bank’s Procurement Regulations for IPF Borrowers - Procurement in Investment Project Financing Goods, Works, Non-Consulting and Consulting Services [July 2016] setting forth the World Bank’s policy on conflict of interest. 

 

Separate EoI's shall be submitted by the firms for each of the assignments.  Interested consultants may apply for the EOI as per the format prescribed (format can be downloaded from the websites www.tenders.tn.gov.in and www.tnuifsl.com) and may obtain further information at the address below during office hours.

 

“Expression of interest must be delivered in Hard copy along with soft copy in a CD to the address below by not later than 27.03.2017, with a superscribing “Empanelling Consultants for various sectors/projects in Tamil Nadu as listed in the notice under TNSUDP – March 2017”.

 

Proposals without superscribing in the cover of the proposals as requested above will be returned unopened.

Please send the EOI in the following order:

 

  1. Covering Letter
  2. Form No.1 – Details of Consultancy Firm
  3. Form No. 2 - Summary of sector wise relevant experience
  4. Form No. 3 - Format for relevant experience:
  5. Form No. 4 - Summary of key professionals available with firm
  6. Form No. 5 – Past five years audited financial reports
  7. Form No. 6 – Any other relevant details
  8. Separate EoI's shall be submitted by the consultancy firm for each of the Sectors / Projects
  9. Separate forms shall be used and submitted by each of the Joint Venture partner(s)
  10. Use separate sheets / additional sheets, as required

 

Empanellement :

 

  1. Separate proposals have to be submitted for each of the sectors.
  2. The consultants will be ranked and empanelled in each sector. As and when assignment comes, the top six firms will be issued RFP. In the case any one of the firm’s declines to participate in submitting their proposals, the next firm in the order of their ranking will be replaced.
  3. Subsequently, for further packages (if any), the next ranked firms will be issued RFP (not necessarily for the Top Six firms) such that all empanelled firms get equal opportunity to participate in the bids.
  4. The firms which have won at-least one award in the mentioned sector will be not included in the subsequent packages.
  5. In the case of firms applying as Joint Venture, both the firm should have atleast minimum one experience in the applied sector. Else, the firm which has experience alone will be empanelled.
  6. In the case of parent company claiming experience of subsidiary firm and vice versa. The empanellement will be done considering both Parent company and subsidiary as a Joint Venture Entity.
  7. In the case of Individuals, only CV’s are required accompanied with the Educational certificates and experience certificates. Firms are also eligible for proposing individuals.

INDIA

Tamil Nadu Sustainable Urban Development Project (P150395)

GENERAL PROCUREMENTNOTICE

 

The Government of India (GoI) has applied for financing in the amount of US$ 400 Million equivalent fromthe World Bank towards the cost of the Tamil Nadu Sustainable Urban Development Project, and it intends to apply part of the proceeds to payments for goods, works, related services and consulting services to be procured under this Project.

 

The Project will include the following components:

 

a)      Urban Investment Component (comprising four sub-components namely Line of Credit Urban Investments, Development of Model Towns, Credit Enhancement and Project Development Advisory Facility) and;

b)      Urban Sector Institutional Development (ID) / Technical Assistance (TA) Component.

Procurement of contracts financed by the World Bank will be conducted through the procedures as specified in the World Bank's Guidelines: Procurement under IBRD Loans and IDACredits January 2011 edition, and is open to all eligible bidders as defined in the guidelines. Consulting services will be selected in accordance with the World Bank's Guidelines: Selection and Employment of Consultants by World Bank Borrowers January 2011 edition.

 

The Project will use manual procurement initially and e-procurement (https://tntenders.gov.in) in due course. Interested bidders should be prepared to use e-procurement system (including obtaining digital certificates and register with the portal).  Project Development Advisory Facility and Technical Assistance Component will use manual procurement procedures.

 

Specific procurement notices for contracts to be bid under the World Bank's International Competitive Bidding (ICB) procedure and for contracts for consultancy services will be announced, as they become available, in UN Development Business andleading National Newspapers. Invitation for bids for National Competitive Bidding (NCB) contracts will be advertised in leading National Newspapers.

 

Interested eligible bidders who wish to be included on the mailing list to receive an invitation to bid under ICB and NCB procedures and interested consultants who wish to receive a copy of the advertisement requesting Expressions of Interest for large-value consultants' contracts, or those requiring additional information, should contact the address below:
